Madras-34, the 26th November 1985 

No, 51-V-34/11/84-Co-ordn.—In persuance of the deci¬ 
sion of the 3(2nd Regional Board, ESl Corporation, Tamil 
Nadu to copatitute Local Committees district-wise, instead 
of area-wise. It has been decided to dissolve the Local
